- **backbone**: The Backbone conductor (ADR-0042) — work out where a tenant stands in its lifecycle, print the position map, propose the next stage with its evidence, and on the operator's go run that existing stage (apply, run, test, publish) in place, then re-derive. Coordination only; writes nothing itself. A tenant id is required (no default tenant). ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone.md))
- **backbone-apply**: Loop-aware apply dispatcher (ADR-0023) — strict batch, no UI clicks. Applies the vault plan via apply-design-plan ({estate root}/{tid}/configs/design_plan.md), then every compiled consumption plan via apply-consumption-plan (configs/consumption/*.md → ledgers → the three generated configs). Deferred consumption plans are named, never auto-compiled. Requires a design plan (run /backbone-plan first). ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-apply.md))
- **backbone-approve**: Give a team approval for a promotion via the backbone-approve skill — shows the DEV evidence for the committed runtime/{tid} subtree hash, asks a named member of the engagement team to reply `approve`, commits approvals/NNN_&lt;date&gt;.yaml on the DEV branch, and prints the push and pull-request commands, running them only on the operator's `push`. Never merges and never touches main. ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-approve.md))
- **backbone-lineage**: Draw a tenant's dbt lineage page from its runtime manifest with the repo's reviewed renderer, publish it to the tenant's one recorded artifact URL, and record what was published. Skips the publish when the page is unchanged. Runs no DML and no dbt; /backbone-run and /backbone-publish call it at their end. On the hosted Backbone the service renders the page and records the publication. ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-lineage.md))
- **backbone-mcp**: Manage a tenant's Claude MCP connector via the backbone-mcp skill — refresh re-grants the read-only connector role SELECT on the published views and their base tables, asserts the grant, refreshes the MCP server when its definitions changed and reports the connector users (DEV only, behind an explicit one-line confirmation; /backbone-publish runs it inside its own confirmation); setup writes the full one-off connector setup script, generated from the declaration, for a Snowflake admin to run as ACCOUNTADMIN; add-user declares a person (a dedicated read-only login or an existing login used onl… ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-mcp.md))
- **backbone-onboard**: Drive a Backbone tenant from nothing (bare Snowflake DB, one source system per schema) to state 1 — connected, in-scope schemas registered as sources, a full source scan written — via the onboard-sources skill, then hand to /backbone-plan. Foreground Socratic HITL; detect-first resume; never touches the private key; works against a local or a hosted backend. ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-onboard.md))
- **backbone-plan**: Loop-aware planning dispatcher (ADR-0023). Classifies a brief into vault intent and consumption intent, confirms the split with the operator, then compiles each half — vault via compile-design-plan ({estate root}/{tid}/configs/design_plan.md), consumption via compile-consumption-plan (configs/consumption/{use_case}.md). Authoring only — execution is /backbone-apply. ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-plan.md))
- **backbone-publish**: Publish/refresh a tenant's governed Snowflake semantic view (scoped dbt run --select tag:semantic_view) via the backbone-publish skill, behind a DEV-only DML approval gate, then run /backbone-mcp &lt;tid&gt; refresh inside the same confirmation — connector-role grants, the live grant assertion, the delegated MCP-server refresh and the connector-users report. DEV requires explicit confirmation; PROD (or any non-DEV target) is refused outright. Requires a prior /backbone-apply (the semantic model must exist). ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-publish.md))
- **backbone-run**: Materialise a built Backbone vault into Snowflake via the backbone-run skill, behind a DML approval gate. DEV requires explicit confirmation; PROD (or any non-DEV target) is refused outright. Requires a prior /backbone-apply (the build must exist). ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-run.md))
- **backbone-test**: Run the already-generated dbt tests for a Backbone tenant via the backbone-test skill — executes existing tests only, never regenerates. Requires a prior /backbone-apply (tests emitted + synced into the runtime dbt repo). ([Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin](https://github.com/Calon-Analytics/backbone-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/backbone-test.md))

- **backlog-refinement**: Provides a structured 'front door' to the beans workflow. Instead of requiring users to already know how to decompose their ideas into well-scoped beans, this command accepts free-form text and guides the user through a conversational refinement process. The Team Lead analyzes the input, asks clarifying questions, and creates properly-formed beans once understanding is clear. - **bam-copilot-loop**: Drive a PR's Copilot review to a clean state, or stop blocked on escalate.
